    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

Prior z-score recovery (0.13→0.68) was with LR, not RF.\"}, \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Z-score standardization has negligible effect on RF cross-strategy LOO. temp09 d1v2 AUC remains inverted: GPT-2 XL 0.1288→0.1348, OLMo 0.2529→0.2497, Pythia 0.576→0.594. Root cause: RF is a tree-based model invariant to monotonic feature transforms — z-score shifts/scales features but preserves relative ordering, so split thresholds adapt automatically. Prior independent verification (GPT-2 XL 0.13→0.68, OLMo 0.25→0.83) was with a scale-sensitive classifier (LR), not RF. Raw results match cross_strategy_loo_001 exactly (mean acc=0.5908). Non-temp09 impact negligible (mean Δd1v2=+0.006). 不是 confound 而是有利条件，移除了关于\\\"所有实验用 IT 模型是否引入系统偏差\\\"的审稿顾虑\\n\\n### EDD Bug 教训\\n- `llama3_eval.py` 硬编码阈值 0.55 而非项目标准 0.70，导致初始 EDD 报告为 \\\">5\\\" 而非正确的 EDD=3\\n- **规则**：任何 eval 脚本中的 EDD 阈值必须参数化并默认   "task_prompt": "## 任务：收集 plan_022 文本长度敏感性分析结果\n\n### 背景\n之前一个 Worker 在 gpu-6000pro-2 服务器上启动了 plan_022 文本长度敏感性分析（CPU 任务），但在结果出来之前就退出了。任务内容是：对已有 3×3 矩阵数据（3 models × 3 strategies × 4 depths），截断到 50/100/200/500 tokens 重新提取 19 个统计特征 + RF 评估，分析 depth estimation accuracy 对文本长度的敏感性。\n\n任务大约在 UTC 12:47 启动，预计 ~38 分钟完成，所以现在应该已经完成。\n\n### 你的任务\n1. SSH 到 gpu-6000pro-2\n2. 找到 plan_022 的输出文件：\n   - 检查 `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/` 下的 results 目录\n   - 搜索关键词：text_length, truncation, sensitivity\n   - 检查最近修改的文件：`find /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/ -name \"*.json\" -o -name \"*.csv\" -o -name \"*.txt\" -o -name \"*.log\" | xargs ls -lt | head -30`\n   - 也检查是否有相关 Python 进程还在跑：`ps aux | grep -i \"text_length\\|truncat\\|sensitivity\"`\n3. 如果任务已完成：\n   - 读取结果文件，提取关键指标（每个截断长度的 4-class acc、d1v2 AUC 等）\n   - 在结果中用 `save_project_file` 写一个简要总结到 memory.md（追加到现有内容，不覆盖）\n   - 用 `update_registry` 创建实验条目 `text_length_sensitivity_001`，status=DONE, conclusion=success/negative\n   - 用 `register_artifact` 注册结果文件\n4. 如果任务还在跑：报告进度和预计完成时间\n5. 如果任务失败/找不到：报告错误信息\n\n### 输出\n汇报找到的完整结果数据（每个 truncation length 的 accuracy、AUC 等），或者失败原因。\n",

{

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：Table 1 Bootstrap Confidence Intervals (plan_020)\n\n### 背景\n项目 recursive_gen_depth_est 研究 ordinal generation depth estimation。3×3 核心矩阵（3 models: Pythia-1.4B, GPT-2 XL, OLMo-1B × 3 strategies: nucleus p=0.95, temp τ=0.9, top-k k=50）已完成，每个 cell 有 RF 4-class accuracy。Dream 审稿三档均要求在 Table 1 中加 bootstrap confidence intervals。\n\n### 现有数据\n服务器 gpu-6000pro-2 上 `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/` 目录下，每个模型/策略组合有提取好的 19 维特征 CSV 文件。数据子目录命名规则：\n- `data/pythia_nucleus095/`, `data/pythia_temp09/`, `data/pythia_topk50/`\n- `data/gpt2xl_nucleus095/`, `data/gpt2xl_temp09/`, `data/gpt2xl_topk50/`\n- `data/olmo_nucleus095/`, `data/olmo_temp09/`, `data/olmo_topk50/`\n\n每个子目录应有 `features_19dim.csv` 或类似名称的特征文件。\n\n### 你的任务\n1. SSH 到 gpu-6000pro-2\n2. 先探索目录结构，找到所有 9 个 cell 的特征数据文件\n3. 写一个 Python 脚本实现 5-fold CV + bootstrap CI：\n   - 对每个 cell：加载特征数据 → 5-fold CV with RF → 在 test predictions 上做 1000 次 bootstrap → 计算 95% CI\n   - 输出：每个 cell 的 mean accuracy 和 [lower, upper] 95% CI\n   - 同时计算 d1v2 AUC 和 d2v3 AUC 的 bootstrap CI\n4. 运行脚本并收集结果\n5. 用 `update_registry` 创建实验 `bootstrap_ci_001`：\n   - hypothesis: \"Bootstrap CIs for 9 matrix cells provide statistical rigor for Table 1 accuracy claims\"\n   - status: DONE\n   - conclusion: success\n   - metrics: 包含所有 9 个 cell 的 acc [CI] 和 AUC [CI]\n6. 用 `register_artifact` 注册结果文件\n\n### 注意\n- 这是纯 CPU 任务，不需要 GPU\n- RF 参数与之前实验一致：n_estimators=100, random_state=42\n- Bootstrap 用 stratified resampling 保持 class balance\n- 如果找不到特征文件，先检查 `results/` 目录下是否有已计算的 fold predictions\n\n### 输出\n报告完整的 9-cell bootstrap CI 结果表。\n",
